p=require('./package.json'); process.stdout.write(p.devDependencies.nx || p.dependencies.nx || '')\")\necho \"nx-version=$NX_VERSION\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"","# Revert renovate's changes to package manifests and the lockfile so that it is a clean migrate from the status quo\ngit checkout HEAD~1 -- package.json package-lock.json packages/lerna/package.json\n\n# Run install and dedupe\n# `npm install` (unlike `npm ci`) is allowed to update the lockfile\nnpm install --ignore-scripts --legacy-peer-deps\nnpm dedupe --legacy-peer-deps\n\nnpx nx migrate \"$NX_VERSION\"\n\n# Sometimes Nx can require config formatting changes after a migrate command\n# We need to expect lock file changes to be applicable\nnpm install --ignore-scripts --legacy-peer-deps\nnpx nx format\n\n# migrations.json may or may not exist after running nx migrate\nif [ -f migrations.json ]; then\n  npx nx migrate --run-migrations=migrations.json\n\n  # After we have run its migrations, we no longer need the migrations.json file\n  rm migrations.json\nfi\n\n# Ensure all the changed files are formatted appropriately\nnpm run format:write\n\ngit add --all\n# Only produce a patch if there are changes to push (nx migrate may be a no-op, see the push_nx_migrate_changes job)\nif git diff --cached --quiet; then\n  echo \"No changes to commit after nx migrate\"\n  echo \"has-changes=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\nelse\n  git diff --cached --binary > \"$RUNNER_TEMP/nx-migrate-changes.patch\"\n  echo \"has-changes=true\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\nfi","# If the PR branch has been updated since the patch was generated this may fail to apply,\n# in which case the workflow will simply run again for the newer commit\ngit apply \"$RUNNER_TEMP/nx-migrate-changes.patch\"\n\ngit add --all\n\n# Commit all the changes to the PR (see note on not being able to use secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN for this)\ngit config --global user.email \"james@henry.sc\"\ngit config --global user.name \"JamesHenry\"\ngit remote set-url origin https://x-access-token:$GITHUB_TOKEN@github.com/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY.git\n\n# SECURITY: --no-verify ensures that no git hooks can run as part of committing and\n# pushing. None should ever be registered in this job because nothing is installed, but\n# this is cheap defence in depth against a malicious hook being registered somehow.\ngit commit --no-verify -m \"chore: run nx migrate for nx v$NX_VERSION\"\ngit push --no-verify origin HEAD"]},{"path":".github/workflows/other-node-versions.yml","commands":["NODE_VERSIONS_JSON_ARRAY=$(node -e attribution cannot be squatted)\ngit config user.name \"github-actions[bot]\"\ngit config user.email \"41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com\"","set -euo pipefail\n\ngit remote set-url origin \"https://x-access-token:${RELEASE_PAT}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git\"\n\nargs=()\nif [[ -n \"$RELEASE_VERSION\" ]]; then\n  # The free-text input must be a single exact semver version, nothing else,\n  # so it cannot smuggle additional flags into the release script\n  if [[ ! \"$RELEASE_VERSION\" =~ ^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(-[0-9A-Za-z-]+(\\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*)?$ ]]; then\n    echo \"version input must be an exact semver version, got: $RELEASE_VERSION\" >&2\n    exit 1\n  fi\n  args+=(\"$RELEASE_VERSION\")\nfi\n\nnpm run lerna-release -- \"${args[@]}\" \\\n  --local false \\\n  --tag \"$RELEASE_TAG\" \\\n  --preid \"$RELEASE_PREID\" \\\n  --clearLocalRegistry false \\\n  --noInteractive","set -euo pipefail\n\nnpx nx build lerna --no-dte\n\nnpx lerna publish from-package ci`) is allowed to update the lockfile\nnpm install --ignore-scripts\nnpm dedupe\n\n# Reset any changes that happened before formatting (e.g. to the lock file) so that we don't commit them\ngit reset --hard\n\n# Update the formatting of all relevant files\nnpm run format:write\n\n# Add everything that changed after the formatting\ngit add --all\n\n# Only produce a patch if there are changes to push (see the push_updated_formatting job)\nif git diff --cached --quiet; then\n  echo \"No formatting changes detected\"\n  echo \"has-changes=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\nelse\n  git diff --cached --all\n\n# Commit all formatting changes to the PR (see note on not being able to use secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN for this)\ngit config --global user.email \"james@henry.sc\"\ngit config --global user.name and\n# pushing. \"is_affected=$IS_AFFECTED\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT","npx nx prepare-for-e2e e2e-run-task-runner --no-cloud","# We do not want the automatic Github Actions grouping to be applied to the e2e tests, or the \"require('./package.json').packageManager\")\" --min-release-age=0 --ignore-scripts","gh pr checkout ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}","NX_VERSION=$(node -e \"const p=require('./package.json'); process.stdout.write(p.devDependencies.nx || p.dependencies.nx || '')\")\necho \"nx-version=$NX_VERSION\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"","# Revert renovate's changes to package manifests and the lockfile so that it is a clean migrate from the status quo\ngit checkout HEAD~1 -- package.json package-lock.json packages/lerna/package.json\n\n# Run install and dedupe\n# `npm install` (unlike `npm ci`) is allowed to update the lockfile\nnpm install --ignore-scripts --legacy-peer-deps\nnpm dedupe --legacy-peer-deps\n\nnpx nx migrate \"$NX_VERSION\"\n\n# Sometimes Nx can require config formatting changes after a migrate command\n# We need to expect lock file changes to be applicable\nnpm install --ignore-scripts --legacy-peer-deps\nnpx nx format\n\n# migrations.json may or may not exist after running nx migrate\nif [ -f migrations.json ]; then\n  npx nx migrate --run-migrations=migrations.json\n\n  # After we have run its migrations, we no longer need the migrations.json file\n  rm migrations.json\nfi\n\n# Ensure all the changed files are formatted appropriately\nnpm run format:write\n\ngit add --all\n# Only produce a patch if there are changes to push (nx migrate may be a no-op, see the push_nx_migrate_changes job)\nif git diff --cached --quiet; then\n  echo \"No changes to commit after nx migrate\"\n  echo \"has-changes=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\nelse\n  git diff --cached --binary > \"$RUNNER_TEMP/nx-migrate-changes.patch\"\n  echo \"has-changes=true\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\nfi","# If the PR branch has been updated since the patch was generated this may fail to apply,\n# in which case the workflow will simply run again for the newer commit\ngit apply \"$RUNNER_TEMP/nx-migrate-changes.patch\"\n\ngit add --all\n\n# Commit all the changes to the PR (see note on not being able to use secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN for this)\ngit config --global user.email \"james@henry.sc\"\ngit config --global user.name \"JamesHenry\"\ngit remote set-url origin https://x-access-token:$GITHUB_TOKEN@github.com/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY.git\n\n# SECURITY: --no-verify ensures that no git hooks can run as part of committing and\n# pushing. None should ever be registered in this job because nothing is installed, but\n# this is cheap defence in depth against a malicious hook being registered somehow.\ngit commit --no-verify -m \"chore: run nx migrate for nx v$NX_VERSION\"\ngit push --no-verify origin HEAD"]},{"path":".github/workflows/other-node-versions.yml","commands":["NODE_VERSIONS_JSON_ARRAY=$(node -e ${{ matrix.node == '22.22.2' && 'linux-node22-lerna' || matrix.node == '24' && 'linux-node24-lerna' || 'unsupported-node' }}\" --stop-agents-after=\"build,test,integration,e2e\" --require-explicit-completion --with-env-vars=\"PUBLISHED_VERSION\"","jq 'del(.volta)' package.json > package.json.tmp\nmv package.json.tmp package.json","corepack enable","corepack install -g \"Tester McPerson\"","npx nx run-many --t e2e --parallel=1","# We do not want the automatic Github Actions grouping to be applied to the e2e tests, or the snapshots won't match local\nunset --ignore-scripts","npm ci --ignore-scripts","npm run build","npx pkg-pr-new publish --compact --peerDeps --no-template --comment=off './packages/lerna'"]},{"path":".github/workflows/release.yml","commands":["# GitHub's reserved Actions bot identity (user ID 41898282 maps to the real\n# github-actions[bot] account, so the attribution cannot be squatted)\ngit config user.name \"github-actions[bot]\"\ngit config user.email \"41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com\"","set -euo pipefail\n\ngit remote set-url origin \"https://x-access-token:${RELEASE_PAT}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git\"\n\nargs=()\nif [[ -n \"$RELEASE_VERSION\" ]]; then\n  # The free-text input must be a single exact semver version, nothing else,\n  # so it cannot smuggle additional flags into the release script\n  if [[ ! \"$RELEASE_VERSION\" =~ ^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(-[0-9A-Za-z-]+(\\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*)?$ ]]; then\n    echo \"version input must be an exact semver version, got: $RELEASE_VERSION\" >&2\n    exit 1\n  fi\n  args+=(\"$RELEASE_VERSION\")\nfi\n\nnpm run lerna-release -- \"${args[@]}\" \\\n  --local false \\\n  --tag \"$RELEASE_TAG\" \\\n  --preid \"$RELEASE_PREID\" \\\n  --clearLocalRegistry false \\\n  --noInteractive","set -euo pipefail\n\nnpx nx build lerna --no-dte\n\nnpx lerna publish from-package --dist-tag \"$RELEASE_TAG\" --yes --loglevel info"]},{"path":".github/workflows/update-formatting-for-renovate.yml","commands":["npm install --global \"$(node -p \"require('./package.json').packageManager\")\" --min-release-age=0 --ignore-scripts","gh pr checkout ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}","# Run install and dedupe\n# `npm install` (unlike `npm ci`) is allowed to update the lockfile\nnpm install --ignore-scripts\nnpm dedupe\n\n# Reset any changes that happened before formatting (e.g. to the lock file) so that we don't commit them\ngit reset --hard\n\n# Update the formatting of all relevant files\nnpm run format:write\n\n# Add everything that changed after the formatting\ngit add --all\n\n# Only produce a patch if there are changes to push (see the push_updated_formatting job)\nif git diff --cached --quiet; then\n  echo \"No formatting changes detected\"\n  echo \"has-changes=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\nelse\n  git diff --cached --binary > \"$RUNNER_TEMP/formatting-changes.patch\"\n  echo \"has-changes=true\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\nfi","# If the PR branch has been updated since the patch was generated this may fail to apply,\n# in which case the workflow will simply run again for the newer commit\ngit apply \"$RUNNER_TEMP/formatting-changes.patch\"\n\n# Add everything that changed after the formatting\ngit add --all\n\n# Commit all formatting changes to the PR (see note on not being able to use secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN for this)\ngit config --global user.email \"james@henry.sc\"\ngit config --global user.name \"JamesHenry\"\ngit remote set-url origin https://x-access-token:$GITHUB_TOKEN@github.com/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY.git\n\n# SECURITY: --no-verify ensures that no git hooks can run as part of committing and\n# pushing.
